;; Header here: 3 screens, level mode 00, tileset 0, sprite GFX 3, music 0, 300 second timer, no scroll restriction.

;; Quirk: extended object 00 (screen exit) is 4 bytes; the extra byte is the destination. Everything after the $FF terminator is unused filler.
